FILE: mob/app/_layout.js
============================================================

     1	import { Stack, usePathname } from "expo-router";
     2	import { useFonts } from "expo-font";
     3	import { Animated, View } from "react-native";
     4	import { useRef } from "react";
     5	
     6	import AppHeader from "../components/AppHeader";
     7	import { HeaderScrollProvider } from "../utils/headerScrollContext";
     8	
     9	export default function RootLayout() {
    10	  const pathname = usePathname();
    11	  const headerScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    12	
    13	  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({
    14	    "Dream Avenue": require("../assets/fonts/dream_avenue/dream_avenue.ttf"),
    15	    "TT Fors": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_regular.ttf"),
    16	  });
    17	
    18	  if (!fontsLoaded) {
    19	    return null;
    20	  }
    21	
    22	  const showPersistentHeader = pathname !== "/shop";
    23	
    24	  return (
    25	    <HeaderScrollProvider scrollY={headerScrollY}>
    26	      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2" }}>
    27	        {showPersistentHeader ? <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} /> : null}
    28	
    29	        <Stack
    30	          screenOptions={{
    31	            headerShown: false,
    32	            contentStyle: {
    33	              backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2",
    34	            },
    35	          }}
    36	        />
    37	      </View>
    38	    </HeaderScrollProvider>
    39	  );
    40	}

     1	import { Animated, Image, Text, View } from "react-native";

     7	
     8	export default function AboutusScreen() {
     9	  const scrollY = useHeaderScrollY();
    10

    20	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}

    64	      "Serving 8 guests, this generous board brings a full Alla Vostra spread to larger gatherings, celebrations, and holiday tables.",
    65	    image: require("../janny3erevised.png"),
    66	    alt: "Alla Vostra Buon Natale grazing board",
    67	    includes: [
    68	      {
    69	        title: "3 Types of Meat",
    70	        amount: "20oz",
    71	        items: ["Salame (Calabrese, Di Parma, Genoa)*", "Prosciutto*", "Coppa"],
    72	        note: "*exact types of meat may vary",
    73	      },
    74	      {
    75	        title: "4 Types of Cheese",
    76	        amount: "24oz",
    77	        items: ["Gouda", "Brie", "Cheddar", "Parmessano"],
    78	      },
    79	      {
    80	        title: "A Selection of Accoutrements",
    81	        amount: "Varies",
    82	        items: ["Fruits", "Nuts*", "Starches", "Jam", "Chocolate"],
    83	        note: "*replaceable in case of allergies",
    84	      },
    85	    ],
    86	  },
    87	];
    88	
    89	function ProductSection({ product }) {
    90	  return (
    91	    <View style={productsStyles.productCard}>
    92	      <View style={productsStyles.productImageWrap}>
    93	        <Image
    94	          source={product.image}
    95	          style={productsStyles.productImage}
    96	          resizeMode="contain"
    97	          accessibilityLabel={product.alt}
    98	        />
    99	      </View>
   100	
   101	      <Text style={productsStyles.productTitle}>{product.title}</Text>
   102	      <Text style={productsStyles.productDescription}>{product.serving}</Text>
   103	
   104	      <View style={productsStyles.productDetails}>
   105	        {product.includes.map((section) => (
   106	          <View key={section.title} style={productsStyles.includeSection}>
   107	            <View style={productsStyles.includeHeaderRow}>
   108	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeTitle}>{section.title}</Text>
   109	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeAmount}>{section.amount}</Text>
   110	            </View>
   111	
   112	            {section.items.map((item) => (
   113	              <Text key={item} style={productsStyles.includeItem}>
   114	                → {item}
   115	              </Text>
   116	            ))}
   117	
   118	            {section.note ? (
   119	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeNote}>{section.note}</Text>
   120	            ) : null}
   121	          </View>
   122	        ))}
   123	      </View>
   124	    </View>
   125	  );
   126	}
   127	
   128	export default function ProductsScreen() {
   129	  const scrollY = useHeaderScrollY();
   130	
   131	  useEffect(() => {
   132	    scrollY?.setValue(0);
   133	  }, [scrollY]);
   134	
   135	  return (
   136	    <View style={productsStyles.screen}>
   137	      <Animated.ScrollView
   138	        style={productsStyles.scroll}
   139	        contentContainerStyle={productsStyles.scrollContent}
   140	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   141	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   142	        onScroll={Animated.event(
   143	          [{ nativeEvent: { contentOffset: { y: scrollY } } }],
   144	          { useNativeDriver: false }
   145	        )}
   146	      >
   147	        <View style={productsStyles.main}>
   148	          <Text style={productsStyles.pageTitle}>Products</Text>
   149	
   150	          <PageDivider />
   151	
   152	          {products.map((product, index) => (
   153	            <View key={product.title}>
   154	              <ProductSection product={product} />
   155	              {index < products.length - 1 ? <PageDivider /> : null}
   156	            </View>
   157	          ))}
   158	        </View>
   159	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   160	    </View>
   161	  );
   162	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/components/AppHeader.js
============================================================

     1	import {
     2	  Animated,
     3	  PanResponder,
     4	  Platform,
     5	  Pressable,
     6	  Text,
     7	  View,
     8	} from "react-native";
     9	import { Link, router, usePathname } from "expo-router";
    10	import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
    11	
    12	import styles from "../styles/headerStyles";
    13	
    14	const navPages = ["home", "products", "aboutus", "contact"];
    15	
    16	const pageLabels = {
    17	  home: "Home",
    18	  products: "Products",
    19	  aboutus: "About Us",
    20	  contact: "Contact",
    21	  shop: "Shop",
    22	};
    23	
    24	const pageRoutes = {
    25	  home: "/",
    26	  products: "/products",
    27	  aboutus: "/aboutus",
    28	  contact: "/contact",
    29	  shop: "/shop",
    30	};
    31	
    32	function getActivePageFromPath(pathname) {
    33	  if (pathname === "/products") return "products";
    34	  if (pathname === "/aboutus") return "aboutus";
    35	  if (pathname === "/contact") return "contact";
    36	  if (pathname === "/shop") return "shop";
    37	  return "home";
    38	}
    39	
    40	export default function AppHeader({
    41	  activePage,
    42	  scrollY,
    43	  showCarousel = true,
    44	  showHero = true,
    45	  showOnlyCarousel = false,
    46	  showOnlyHero = false,
    47	}) {
    48	  const pathname = usePathname();
    49	  const resolvedActivePage = activePage || getActivePageFromPath(pathname);
    50	
    51	  const fallbackScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    52	  const safeScrollY = scrollY || fallbackScrollY;
    53	
    54	  const leftArrowX = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    55	  const rightArrowX = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    56	  const arrowOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0.15)).current;
    57	  const stickyPadding = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    58	  const arrowLoopRef = useRef(null);
    59	  const wasAtTopRef = useRef(true);
    60	  const isStickyRef = useRef(false);
    61	
    62	  const currentNavIndex = Math.max(navPages.indexOf(resolvedActivePage), 0);
    63	
    64	  const goToPage = (pageName) => {
    65	    if (pageName === resolvedActivePage) return;
    66	
    67	    const route = pageRoutes[pageName];
    68	    if (route) router.push(route);
    69	  };
    70	
    71	  const goToPreviousPage = () => {
    72	    const previousIndex =
    73	      (currentNavIndex + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
    74	
    75	    goToPage(navPages[previousIndex]);
    76	  };
    77	
    78	  const goToNextPage = () => {
    79	    const nextIndex = (currentNavIndex + 1) % navPages.length;
    80	
    81	    goToPage(navPages[nextIndex]);
    82	  };
    83	
    84	  const carouselPanResponder = useRef(
    85	    PanResponder.create({
    86	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: (_, gestureState) =>
    87	        Math.abs(gestureState.dx) > 22 &&
    88	        Math.abs(gestureState.dx) > Math.abs(gestureState.dy),
    89	
    90	      onPanResponderRelease: (_, gestureState) => {
    91	        if (gestureState.dx <= -45) {
    92	          goToNextPage();
    93	        }
    94	
    95	        if (gestureState.dx >= 45) {
    96	          goToPreviousPage();
    97	        }
    98	      },
    99	    })
   100	  ).current;
   101	
   102	  useEffect(() => {
   103	    const startArrowLoop = () => {
   104	      if (arrowLoopRef.current) arrowLoopRef.current.stop();
   105	
   106	      leftArrowX.setValue(0);
   107	      rightArrowX.setValue(0);
   108	      arrowOpacity.setValue(0.15);
   109	
   110	      arrowLoopRef.current = Animated.loop(
   111	        Animated.sequence([
   112	          Animated.parallel([
   113	            Animated.timing(leftArrowX, {
   114	              toValue: 0,
   115	              duration: 0,
   116	              useNativeDriver: true,
   117	            }),
   118	            Animated.timing(rightArrowX, {
   119	              toValue: 0,
   120	              duration: 0,
   121	              useNativeDriver: true,
   122	            }),
   123	            Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   124	              toValue: 0.15,
   125	              duration: 0,
   126	              useNativeDriver: true,
   127	            }),
   128	          ]),
   129	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   130	            toValue: 0.45,
   131	            duration: 490,
   132	            useNativeDriver: true,
   133	          }),
   134	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   135	            toValue: 0.15,
   136	            duration: 490,
   137	            useNativeDriver: true,
   138	          }),
   139	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   140	            toValue: 0.45,
   141	            duration: 490,
   142	            useNativeDriver: true,
   143	          }),
   144	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   145	            toValue: 0.15,
   146	            duration: 490,
   147	            useNativeDriver: true,
   148	          }),
   149	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   150	            toValue: 0.45,
   151	            duration: 490,
   152	            useNativeDriver: true,
   153	          }),
   154	          Animated.parallel([
   155	            Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   156	              toValue: 0,
   157	              duration: 665,
   158	              useNativeDriver: true,
   159	            }),
   160	            Animated.timing(leftArrowX, {
   161	              toValue: -120,
   162	              duration: 665,
   163	              useNativeDriver: true,
   164	            }),
   165	            Animated.timing(rightArrowX, {
   166	              toValue: 120,
   167	              duration: 665,
   168	              useNativeDriver: true,
   169	            }),
   170	          ]),
   171	          Animated.delay(500),
   172	        ])
   173	      );
   174	
   175	      arrowLoopRef.current.start();
   176	    };
   177	
   178	    const stopArrowLoop = () => {
   179	      if (arrowLoopRef.current) {
   180	        arrowLoopRef.current.stop();
   181	        arrowLoopRef.current = null;
   182	      }
   183	
   184	      leftArrowX.setValue(0);
   185	      rightArrowX.setValue(0);
   186	      arrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   187	    };
   188	
   189	    const animateStickyPadding = (toValue) => {
   190	      Animated.timing(stickyPadding, {
   191	        toValue,
   192	        duration: 90,
   193	        useNativeDriver: false,
   194	      }).start();
   195	    };
   196	
   197	    startArrowLoop();
   198	
   199	    const listenerId = safeScrollY.addListener(({ value }) => {
   200	      const isAtTop = value <= 1;
   201	      const shouldBeSticky = value >= 120 && Platform.OS !== "web";
   202	
   203	      if (shouldBeSticky !== isStickyRef.current) {
   204	        isStickyRef.current = shouldBeSticky;
   205	        animateStickyPadding(shouldBeSticky ? 20 : 0);
   206	      }
   207	
   208	      if (!isAtTop && wasAtTopRef.current) {
   209	        wasAtTopRef.current = false;
   210	        stopArrowLoop();
   211	      }
   212	
   213	      if (isAtTop && !wasAtTopRef.current) {
   214	        wasAtTopRef.current = true;
   215	        startArrowLoop();
   216	      }
   217	    });
   218	
   219	    return () => {
   220	      stopArrowLoop();
   221	      safeScrollY.removeListener(listenerId);
   222	    };
   223	  }, [arrowOpacity, leftArrowX, rightArrowX, safeScrollY, stickyPadding]);
   224	
   225	  const activeLink = pageLabels[resolvedActivePage] || "Home";
   226	
   227	  const arrowPlacementOpacity = safeScrollY.interpolate({
   228	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   229	    outputRange: [1, 0],
   230	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   231	  });
   232	
   233	  const visibleArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   234	    arrowOpacity,
   235	    arrowPlacementOpacity
   236	  );
   237	
   238	  const stickyHeight =
   239	    Platform.OS === "web" ? 84 : Animated.add(84, stickyPadding);
   240	
   241	  const centerShadowOpacity =
   242	    Platform.OS === "web"
   243	      ? 1
   244	      : stickyPadding.interpolate({
   245	          inputRange: [0, 20],
   246	          outputRange: [0, 1],
   247	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   248	        });
   249	
   250	  const heroScale = safeScrollY.interpolate({
   251	    inputRange: [0, 500],
   252	    outputRange: [1.5, 3.05],
   253	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   254	  });
   255	
   256	  const heroTranslateY = safeScrollY.interpolate({
   257	    inputRange: [0, 500],
   258	    outputRange: [30, -56],
   259	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   260	  });
   261	
   262	  const headerTranslateY = safeScrollY.interpolate({
   263	    inputRange: [0, 120],
   264	    outputRange: [0, -120],
   265	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   266	  });
   267	
   268	  const carousel = (
   269	    <View style={styles.carouselShell} {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}>
   270	      <Animated.View
   271	        style={[
   272	          styles.carouselNavBar,
   273	          Platform.OS !== "web"
   274	            ? {
   275	                height: stickyHeight,
   276	                paddingTop: stickyPadding,
   277	              }
   278	            : null,
   279	        ]}
   280	      >
   281	        <View style={styles.carouselInner}>
   282	          <Pressable onPress={goToPreviousPage}>
   283	            <Animated.View
   284	              style={[
   285	                styles.arrowBox,
   286	                {
   287	                  opacity: visibleArrowOpacity,
   288	                  transform: [{ translateX: leftArrowX }],
   289	                },
   290	              ]}
   291	            >
   292	              <View style={[styles.arrowChevron, styles.arrowChevronLeft]} />
   293	            </Animated.View>
   294	          </Pressable>
   295	
   296	          <Pressable onPress={goToNextPage}>
   297	            <Text style={styles.carouselActiveText}>{activeLink}</Text>
   298	          </Pressable>
   299	
   300	          <Pressable onPress={goToNextPage}>
   301	            <Animated.View
   302	              style={[
   303	                styles.arrowBox,
   304	                {
   305	                  opacity: visibleArrowOpacity,
   306	                  transform: [{ translateX: rightArrowX }],
   307	                },
   308	              ]}
   309	            >
   310	              <View style={[styles.arrowChevron, styles.arrowChevronRight]} />
   311	            </Animated.View>
   312	          </Pressable>
   313	        </View>
   314	      </Animated.View>
   315	
   316	      <Animated.View
   317	        pointerEvents="none"
   318	        style={[
   319	          styles.carouselCenterShadow,
   320	          {
   321	            opacity: centerShadowOpacity,
   322	          },
   323	        ]}
   324	      >
   325	        {Platform.OS !== "web" && (
   326	          <>
   327	            {Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, index) => (
   328	              <View
   329	                key={`center-shadow-layer-${index + 1}`}
   330	                style={styles[`centerShadowLayer${index + 1}`]}
   331	              />
   332	            ))}
   333	          </>
   334	        )}
   335	      </Animated.View>
   336	    </View>
   337	  );
   338	
   339	  const hero = (
   340	    <View style={styles.hero}>
   341	      <Animated.Image
   342	        source={require("../background3.png")}
   343	        style={[
   344	          styles.heroImage,
   345	          {
   346	            transform: [{ scale: heroScale }, { translateY: heroTranslateY }],
   347	          },
   348	        ]}
   349	        resizeMode="cover"
   350	      />
   351	    </View>
   352	  );
   353	
   354	  if (showOnlyCarousel) return carousel;
   355	  if (showOnlyHero) return hero;
   356	
   357	  return (
   358	    <Animated.View
   359	      style={[
   360	        styles.header,
   361	        {
   362	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
   363	        },
   364	      ]}
   365	    >
   366	      <View style={styles.orangeBar}>
   367	        <Link href="/" asChild>
   368	          <Pressable style={styles.logoPressable}>
   369	            <Text style={styles.logoText}>Alla Vostra</Text>
   370	          </Pressable>
   371	        </Link>
   372	
   373	        <View style={styles.shopButtonWrap}>
   374	          <Link href="/shop" asChild>
   375	            <Pressable style={styles.shopButton}>
   376	              <Text style={styles.shopButtonText}>SHOP</Text>
   377	            </Pressable>
   378	          </Link>
   379	        </View>
   380	      </View>
   381	
   382	      {showCarousel && carousel}
   383	      {showHero && hero}
   384	    </Animated.View>
   385	  );
   386	}

     1	<html>

FILE: mob/index-old.html
============================================================

     1	<script>
     2	
     3	const parallax =
     4	  document.querySelector(".parallax");
     5	
     6	const navbar =
     7	  document.getElementById("indexTable");
     8	
     9	let frozen = false;
    10	
    11	function updateFreeze() {
    12	
    13	  if (!parallax || !navbar) return;
    14	
    15	  const navbarRect =
    16	    navbar.getBoundingClientRect();
    17	
    18	  const navbarBottom =
    19	    navbarRect.bottom;
    20	
    21	  /*
    22	    freeze exactly when navbar
    23	    leaves viewport
    24	  */
    25	
    26	  if (navbarBottom <= 0 && !frozen) {
    27	
    28	    frozen = true;
    29	
    30	    /*
    31	      capture current visible state
    32	    */
    33	
    34	    const rect =
    35	      parallax.getBoundingClientRect();
    36	
    37	    /*
    38	      permanently pin hero/banner
    39	    */
    40	
    41	    parallax.style.position = "fixed";
    42	
    43	    parallax.style.top =
    44	      rect.top + "px";
    45	
    46	    parallax.style.left =
    47	      rect.left + "px";
    48	
    49	    parallax.style.width =
    50	      rect.width + "px";
    51	
    52	    parallax.style.height =
    53	      rect.height + "px";
    54	
    55	    parallax.style.margin = "0";
    56	
    57	    parallax.style.zIndex = "1";
    58	
    59	    parallax.style.pointerEvents =
    60	      "none";
    61	
    62	    /*
    63	      freeze all transforms
    64	    */
    65	
    66	    parallax
    67	      .querySelectorAll("*")
    68	      .forEach(el => {
    69	
    70	        const computed =
    71	          getComputedStyle(el).transform;
    72	
    73	        if (
    74	          computed &&
    75	          computed !== "none"
    76	        ) {
    77	          el.style.transform =
    78	            computed;
    79	        }
    80	
    81	      });
    82	
    83	    parallax.classList.add("frozen");
    84	
    85	  }
    86	
    87	}
    88	
    89	/*
    90	  disable ALL further animations/transitions
    91	*/
    92	
    93	const style =
    94	  document.createElement("style");
    95	
    96	style.innerHTML = `
    97	
    98	.parallax.frozen *,
    99	.parallax.frozen svg,
   100	.parallax.frozen div {
   101	
   102	  animation: none !important;
   103	
   104	  transition: none !important;
   105	
   106	  will-change: auto !important;
   107	
   108	}
   109	
   110	`;
   111	
   112	document.head.appendChild(style);
   113	
   114	/*
   115	  monitor scrolling
   116	*/
   117	
   118	window.addEventListener(
   119	  "scroll",
   120	  updateFreeze,
   121	  { passive: true }
   122	);
   123	
   124	/*
   125	  original autoscroll behavior
   126	*/
   127	
   128	window.addEventListener("click", () => {
   129	
   130	  window.scrollTo({
   131	    top: 450,
   132	    behavior: "smooth",
   133	  });
   134	
   135	});
   136	
   137	</script>

FILE: mob/script.js

     1	"use strict";
     2	//  passive events 
     3	let passiveSupported = false;
     4	try {
     5	    window.addEventListener("test", null, Object.defineProperty({}, "passive", { get: function () { passiveSupported = true; } }));
     6	}
     7	catch (err) { }
     8	//  scroll listener
     9	addEventListener('scroll', e => {
    10	    const height = document.querySelector('.parallax').getBoundingClientRect().height;
    11	    const percentage = Math.min(Math.max(pageYOffset / height, 0), 1);
    12	    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--pct', percentage);
    13	}, passiveSupported ? { passive: true } : false);
    14	//  link to open without iframe 
    15	const inIframe = () => { try {
    16	    return window.self !== window.top;
    17	}
    18	catch (e) {
    19	    return true;
    20	} };
    21	if (inIframe())
    22	    document.querySelector('.no-iframe').style.display = 'block';
    23	
    24	
    25	
    26	
    27	// contact form
    28	
    29	const form = document.getElementById("form");
    30	const result = document.getElementById("result");
    31	
    32	form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    33	  const formData = new FormData(form);
    34	  e.preventDefault();
    35	  var object = {};
    36	  formData.forEach((value, key) => {
    37	    object[key] = value;
    38	  });
    39	  var json = JSON.stringify(object);
    40	  result.innerHTML = "Please wait...";
    41	
    42	  fetch("https://api.web3forms.com/submit", {
    43	    method: "POST",
    44	    headers: {
    45	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    46	      Accept: "application/json"
    47	    },
    48	    body: json
    49	  })
    50	    .then(async (response) => {
    51	      let json = await response.json();
    52	      if (response.status == 200) {
    53	        result.innerHTML = json.message;
    54	        result.classList.remove("text-gray-500");
    55	        result.classList.add("text-green-500");
    56	      } else {
    57	        console.log(response);
    58	        result.innerHTML = json.message;
    59	        result.classList.remove("text-gray-500");
    60	        result.classList.add("text-red-500");
    61	      }
    62	    })
    63	    .catch((error) => {
    64	      console.log(error);
    65	      result.innerHTML = "Something went wrong!";
    66	    })
    67	    .then(function () {
    68	      form.reset();
    69	      setTimeout(() => {
    70	        result.style.display = "none";
    71	      }, 5000);
    72	    });
    73	});
    74	
    75	// end of contact form
    76

FILE: mob/shop.js
============================================================

     1	if (document.readyState == 'loading') {
     2	    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ready)
     3	} else {
     4	    ready()
     5	}
     6	
     7	function ready() {
     8	    var removeCartItemButtons = document.getElementsByClassName('btn-danger')
     9	    for (var i = 0; i < removeCartItemButtons.length; i++) {
    10	        var button = removeCartItemButtons[i]
    11	        button.addEventListener('click', removeCartItem)
    12	    }
    13	
    14	    var quantityInputs = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')
    15	    for (var i = 0; i < quantityInputs.length; i++) {
    16	        var input = quantityInputs[i]
    17	        input.addEventListener('change', quantityChanged)
    18	    }
    19	
    20	    var addToCartButtons = document.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-button')
    21	    for (var i = 0; i < addToCartButtons.length; i++) {
    22	        var button = addToCartButtons[i]
    23	        button.addEventListener('click', addToCartClicked)
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    document.getElementsByClassName('btn-purchase')[0].addEventListener('click', purchaseClicked)
    27	}
    28	
    29	function purchaseClicked() {
    30	    alert('Thank you for your purchase')
    31	    var cartItems = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    32	    while (cartItems.hasChildNodes()) {
    33	        cartItems.removeChild(cartItems.firstChild)
    34	    }
    35	    updateCartTotal()
    36	}
    37	
    38	function removeCartItem(event) {
    39	    var buttonClicked = event.target
    40	    buttonClicked.parentElement.parentElement.remove()
    41	    updateCartTotal()
    42	}
    43	
    44	function quantityChanged(event) {
    45	    var input = event.target
    46	    if (isNaN(input.value) || input.value <= 0) {
    47	        input.value = 1
    48	    }
    49	    updateCartTotal()
    50	}
    51	
    52	function addToCartClicked(event) {
    53	    var button = event.target
    54	    var shopItem = button.parentElement.parentElement
    55	    var title = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-title')[0].innerText
    56	    var price = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-price')[0].innerText
    57	    var imageSrc = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-image')[0].src
    58	    addItemToCart(title, price, imageSrc)
    59	    updateCartTotal()
    60	}
    61	
    62	function addItemToCart(title, price, imageSrc) {
    63	    var cartRow = document.createElement('div')
    64	    cartRow.classList.add('cart-row')
    65	    var cartItems = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    66	    var cartItemNames = cartItems.getElementsByClassName('cart-item-title')
    67	    for (var i = 0; i < cartItemNames.length; i++) {
    68	        if (cartItemNames[i].innerText == title) {
    69	            alert('This item is already added to the cart')
    70	            return
    71	        }
    72	    }
    73	    var cartRowContents = `
    74	        <div class="cart-item cart-column">
    75	            <img class="cart-item-image" src="${imageSrc}" width="100" height="100">
    76	            <span class="cart-item-title">${title}</span>
    77	        </div>
    78	        <span class="cart-price cart-column">${price}</span>
    79	        <div class="cart-quantity cart-column">
    80	            <input class="cart-quantity-input" type="number" value="1">
    81	            <button class="btn btn-danger" type="button">REMOVE</button>
    82	        </div>`
    83	    cartRow.innerHTML = cartRowContents
    84	    cartItems.append(cartRow)
    85	    cartRow.getElementsByClassName('btn-danger')[0].addEventListener('click', removeCartItem)
    86	    cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')[0].addEventListener('change', quantityChanged)
    87	}
    88	
    89	function updateCartTotal() {
    90	    var cartItemContainer = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    91	    var cartRows = cartItemContainer.getElementsByClassName('cart-row')
    92	    var total = 0
    93	    for (var i = 0; i < cartRows.length; i++) {
    94	        var cartRow = cartRows[i]
    95	        var priceElement = cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-price')[0]
    96	        var quantityElement = cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')[0]
    97	        var price = parseFloat(priceElement.innerText.replace('$', ''))
    98	        var quantity = quantityElement.value
    99	        total = total + (price * quantity)
   100	    }
   101	    total = Math.round(total * 100) / 100
   102	    document.getElementsByClassName('cart-total-price')[0].innerText = '$' + total
   103	}

     1	import { Platform, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	const logoFont = "Dream Avenue";

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/headerScrollContext.js
============================================================

     1	import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
     2	
     3	const HeaderScrollContext = createContext(null);
     4	
     5	export function HeaderScrollProvider({ children, scrollY }) {
     6	  return (
     7	    <HeaderScrollContext.Provider value={scrollY}>
     8	      {children}
     9	    </HeaderScrollContext.Provider>
    10	  );
    11	}
    12	
    13	export function useHeaderScrollY() {
    14	  return useContext(HeaderScrollContext);
    15	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/openPaymentLink.js
============================================================

     1	import { Alert, Linking, Platform } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	export async function openPaymentLink(url) {
     4	  if (!url) {
     5	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
     6	      window.alert("No payment link has been added for this product yet.");
     7	      return;
     8	    }
     9	
    10	    Alert.alert("Payment link missing", "No payment link has been added yet.");
    11	    return;
    12	  }
    13	
    14	  try {
    15	    const canOpen = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
    16	
    17	    if (!canOpen) {
    18	      throw new Error("Cannot open URL");
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    await Linking.openURL(url);
    22	  } catch (error) {
    23	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
    24	      window.alert("Unable to open this payment link.");
    25	      return;
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    Alert.alert("Unable to open link", "Please try again later.");
    29	  }
    30	}

    67	const heroImage = document.querySelector(".hero-image");
    68	const root = document.documentElement;
    69	
    70	function updateHeroScale() {
    71	  if (!heroImage) {
    72	    return;
    73	  }
    74	
    75	  const scrollY = window.scrollY || window.pageYOffset;
    76	  const maxScroll = 520;
    77	  const maxScale = 2.0;
    78	  const maxMainLift = -260;
    79	  const progress = Math.min(scrollY / maxScroll, 1);
    80	  const scale = 1 + progress * (maxScale - 1);
    81	  const mainShift = progress * maxMainLift;
    82	
    83	  heroImage.style.transform = `scale(${scale})`;
    84	  root.style.setProperty("--main-scroll-shift", `${mainShift}px`);
    85	}
    86	
    87	window.addEventListener("scroll", updateHeroScale, { passive: true });
    88	window.addEventListener("resize", updateHeroScale);
    89	updateHeroScale();
    90	
    91	const mobileAnnouncement = document.querySelector(".announcement");
    92	const mobileNav = document.querySelector(".nav-mobile");
    93	
    94	function updateMobileStickyHeader() {
    95	  if (!mobileAnnouncement || !mobileNav) {
    96	    return;
    97	  }
    98	
    99	  if (window.innerWidth > 760) {
   100	    root.style.removeProperty("--mobile-nav-top");
   101	    return;
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  const announcementBottom = mobileAnnouncement.getBoundingClientRect().bottom;
   105	  const navTop = Math.max(0, announcementBottom - 6);
   106	
   107	  root.style.setProperty("--mobile-nav-top", `${navTop}px`);
   108	}
   109	
   110	window.addEventListener("scroll", updateMobileStickyHeader, { passive: true });
   111	window.addEventListener("resize", updateMobileStickyHeader);
   112	updateMobileStickyHeader();
   113	
   114	const mobileNavLinks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".mobile-carousel-link"));
   115	const mobileCarouselWindow = document.querySelector(".mobile-carousel-window");
   116	const mobileCarouselTrack = document.querySelector(".mobile-carousel-track");
   117	
   118	let mobileNavIndex = 0;
   119	let mobileDragStartX = 0;
   120	let mobileDragCurrentX = 0;
   121	let mobileDragging = false;
   122	
   123	function getMobileNavIndexFromPath() {
   124	  const path = window.location.pathname.split("/").pop();
   125	
   126	  if (path === "aboutus.html") {
   127	    return 1;
   128	  }
   129	
   130	  if (path === "contact.html") {
   131	    return 2;
   132	  }
   133	
   134	  return 0;
   135	}
   136	
   137	function setMobileDragOffset(value) {
   138	  if (!mobileCarouselTrack) {
   139	    return;
   140	  }
   141	
   142	  mobileCarouselTrack.style.setProperty("--mobile-drag-offset", `${value}px`);
   143	}
   144	
   145	function renderMobileNav() {
   146	  if (!mobileNavLinks.length) {
   147	    return;
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  mobileNavLinks.forEach((link, index) => {
   151	    link.classList.remove("is-left", "is-center", "is-right", "is-hidden");
   152	
   153	    if (index === mobileNavIndex) {
   154	      link.classList.add("is-center");
   155	      return;
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    if (index === mobileNavIndex - 1) {
   159	      link.classList.add("is-left");
   160	      return;
   161	    }
   162	
   163	    if (index === mobileNavIndex + 1) {
   164	      link.classList.add("is-right");
   165	      return;
   166	    }
   167	
   168	    link.classList.add("is-hidden");
   169	  });
   170	
   171	  setMobileDragOffset(0);
   172	}
   173	
   174	function moveMobileNav(direction) {
   175	  const nextIndex = mobileNavIndex + direction;
   176	
   177	  if (nextIndex < 0 || nextIndex >= mobileNavLinks.length) {
   178	    return;
   179	  }
   180	
   181	  const nextLink = mobileNavLinks[nextIndex];
   182	
   183	  if (nextLink && nextLink.href) {
   184	    window.location.href = nextLink.href;
   185	  }
   186	}
   187	
   188	if (mobileNavLinks.length) {
   189	  mobileNavIndex = getMobileNavIndexFromPath();
   190	  renderMobileNav();
   191	}
   192	
   193	if (mobileCarouselWindow) {
   194	  let mobileDidDrag = false;
   195	
   196	  mobileCarouselWindow.addEventListener("pointerdown", (event) => {
   197	    mobileDragging = true;
   198	    mobileDidDrag = false;
   199	    mobileDragStartX = event.clientX;
   200	    mobileDragCurrentX = event.clientX;
   201	
   202	    mobileCarouselWindow.classList.add("is-dragging");
   203	    mobileCarouselWindow.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
   204	  });
   205	
   206	  mobileCarouselWindow.addEventListener("pointermove", (event) => {
   207	    if (!mobileDragging) {
   208	      return;
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    mobileDragCurrentX = event.clientX;
   212	
   213	    const dragDistance = mobileDragCurrentX - mobileDragStartX;
   214	
   215	    if (Math.abs(dragDistance) > 6) {
   216	      mobileDidDrag = true;
   217	    }
   218	
   219	    setMobileDragOffset(dragDistance);
   220	  });
   221	
   222	  mobileCarouselWindow.addEventListener("pointerup", (event) => {
   223	    if (!mobileDragging) {
   224	      return;
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    const dragDistance = mobileDragCurrentX - mobileDragStartX;
   228	
   229	    mobileDragging = false;
   230	    mobileCarouselWindow.classList.remove("is-dragging");
   231	
   232	    if (Math.abs(dragDistance) > 55) {
   233	      event.preventDefault();
   234	      moveMobileNav(dragDistance < 0 ? 1 : -1);
   235	    } else {
   236	      setMobileDragOffset(0);
   237	    }
   238	
   239	    mobileCarouselWindow.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);
   240	  });
   241	
   242	  mobileCarouselWindow.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
   243	    if (mobileDidDrag) {
   244	      event.preventDefault();
   245	      event.stopPropagation();
   246	      mobileDidDrag = false;
   247	    }
   248	  }, true);
   249	
   250	  mobileCarouselWindow.addEventListener("pointercancel", () => {
   251	    mobileDragging = false;
   252	    mobileDidDrag = false;
   253	    mobileCarouselWindow.classList.remove("is-dragging");
   254	    setMobileDragOffset(0);
   255	  });
   256	
   257	  mobileCarouselWindow.addEventListener(
   258	    "wheel",
   259	    (event) => {
   260	      if (Math.abs(event.deltaX) <= Math.abs(event.deltaY)) {
   261	        return;
   262	      }
   263	
   264	      event.preventDefault();
   265	      moveMobileNav(event.deltaX > 0 ? 1 : -1);
   266	    },
   267	    { passive: false }
   268	  );
   269	}

    95	  <script>
    96	    const slideshows = {
    97	      passion: [
    98	        "assets/images/passion1.jpg",
    99	        "assets/images/passion2.jpg",
   100	        "assets/images/passion3.jpg",
   101	      ],
   102	      taste: [
   103	        "assets/images/taste1.jpg",
   104	        "assets/images/taste2.jpg",
   105	        "assets/images/taste3.jpg",
   106	      ],
   107	      convenience: [
   108	        "assets/images/convenience1.jpg",
   109	        "assets/images/convenience2.jpg",
   110	        "assets/images/convenience111.png",
   111	      ],
   112	    };
   113	
   114	    const slideshowTimers = new Map();
   115	    const slideshowFadeTimers = new Map();
   116	    const slideshowIndexes = new Map();
   117	    const slideshowActive = new Map();
   118	
   119	    function preloadImage(src) {
   120	      return new Promise((resolve) => {
   121	        const img = new Image();
   122	
   123	        img.onload = () => resolve();
   124	        img.onerror = () => resolve();
   125	
   126	        img.src = src;
   127	      });
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    document.querySelectorAll("[data-slideshow]").forEach((baseImage) => {
   131	      const slideshowName = baseImage.dataset.slideshow;
   132	      const slides = slideshows[slideshowName];
   133	
   134	      if (!slides || slides.length === 0) {
   135	        return;
   136	      }
   137	
   138	      const imageParent = baseImage.parentElement;
   139	      let fadeImage = imageParent.querySelector(".slideshow-fade-next");
   140	
   141	      if (!fadeImage) {
   142	        fadeImage = document.createElement("img");
   143	        fadeImage.alt = baseImage.alt;
   144	        fadeImage.className = "slideshow-fade-next";
   145	        imageParent.appendChild(fadeImage);
   146	      }
   147	
   148	      baseImage.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => {
   149	        clearTimeout(slideshowTimers.get(baseImage));
   150	        clearTimeout(slideshowFadeTimers.get(baseImage));
   151	
   152	        slideshowActive.set(baseImage, true);
   153	
   154	        const currentIndex = slides.indexOf(baseImage.getAttribute("src"));
   155	        slideshowIndexes.set(baseImage, currentIndex >= 0 ? currentIndex : 0);
   156	
   157	        async function runNextSlide() {
   158	          if (!slideshowActive.get(baseImage)) {
   159	            return;
   160	          }
   161	
   162	          const currentIndex = slideshowIndexes.get(baseImage);
   163	          const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % slides.length;
   164	          const nextSrc = slides[nextIndex];
   165	
   166	          await preloadImage(nextSrc);
   167	
   168	          if (!slideshowActive.get(baseImage)) {
   169	            return;
   170	          }
   171	
   172	          fadeImage.classList.remove("is-visible");
   173	          fadeImage.src = nextSrc;
   174	
   175	          requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   176	            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   177	              if (!slideshowActive.get(baseImage)) {
   178	                return;
   179	              }
   180	
   181	              fadeImage.classList.add("is-visible");
   182	            });
   183	          });
   184	
   185	          const fadeTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   186	            if (!slideshowActive.get(baseImage)) {
   187	              return;
   188	            }
   189	
   190	            baseImage.src = nextSrc;
   191	            slideshowIndexes.set(baseImage, nextIndex);
   192	
   193	            fadeImage.classList.remove("is-visible");
   194	
   195	            const nextTimer = setTimeout(runNextSlide, 1000);
   196	            slideshowTimers.set(baseImage, nextTimer);
   197	          }, 1000);
   198	
   199	          slideshowFadeTimers.set(baseImage, fadeTimer);
   200	        }
   201	
   202	        const firstTimer = setTimeout(runNextSlide, 1000);
   203	        slideshowTimers.set(baseImage, firstTimer);
   204	      });
   205	
   206	      baseImage.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => {
   207	        slideshowActive.set(baseImage, false);
   208	
   209	        clearTimeout(slideshowTimers.get(baseImage));
   210	        clearTimeout(slideshowFadeTimers.get(baseImage));
   211	
   212	        slideshowTimers.delete(baseImage);
   213	        slideshowFadeTimers.delete(baseImage);
   214	
   215	        const visibleFadeSrc =
   216	          fadeImage.classList.contains("is-visible") && fadeImage.getAttribute("src")
   217	            ? fadeImage.getAttribute("src")
   218	            : null;
   219	
   220	        if (visibleFadeSrc) {
   221	          baseImage.src = visibleFadeSrc;
   222	
   223	          const currentIndex = slides.indexOf(visibleFadeSrc);
   224	          slideshowIndexes.set(baseImage, currentIndex >= 0 ? currentIndex : 0);
   225	        } else {
   226	          const currentIndex = slides.indexOf(baseImage.getAttribute("src"));
   227	          slideshowIndexes.set(baseImage, currentIndex >= 0 ? currentIndex : 0);
   228	        }
   229	
   230	        fadeImage.classList.remove("is-visible");
   231	        fadeImage.removeAttribute("src");
   232	      });
   233	    });
   234	  </script>
   235	</body>
   236	</html>

     1	"use strict";
     2	//  passive events 
     3	let passiveSupported = false;
     4	try {
     5	    window.addEventListener("test", null, Object.defineProperty({}, "passive", { get: function () { passiveSupported = true; } }));
     6	}
     7	catch (err) { }
     8	//  scroll listener
     9	addEventListener('scroll', e => {
    10	    const height = document.querySelector('.parallax').getBoundingClientRect().height;
    11	    const percentage = Math.min(Math.max(pageYOffset / height, 0), 1);
    12	    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--pct', percentage);
    13	}, passiveSupported ? { passive: true } : false);
    14	//  link to open without iframe 
    15	const inIframe = () => { try {
    16	    return window.self !== window.top;
    17	}
    18	catch (e) {
    19	    return true;
    20	} };
    21	if (inIframe())
    22	    document.querySelector('.no-iframe').style.display = 'block';
    23	
    24	
    25	
    26	
    27	// contact form
    28	
    29	const form = document.getElementById("form");
    30	const result = document.getElementById("result");
    31	
    32	form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    33	  const formData = new FormData(form);
    34	  e.preventDefault();
    35	  var object = {};
    36	  formData.forEach((value, key) => {
    37	    object[key] = value;
    38	  });
    39	  var json = JSON.stringify(object);
    40	  result.innerHTML = "Please wait...";
    41	
    42	  fetch("https://api.web3forms.com/submit", {
    43	    method: "POST",
    44	    headers: {
    45	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    46	      Accept: "application/json"
    47	    },
    48	    body: json
    49	  })
    50	    .then(async (response) => {
    51	      let json = await response.json();
    52	      if (response.status == 200) {
    53	        result.innerHTML = json.message;
    54	        result.classList.remove("text-gray-500");
    55	        result.classList.add("text-green-500");
    56	      } else {
    57	        console.log(response);
    58	        result.innerHTML = json.message;
    59	        result.classList.remove("text-gray-500");
    60	        result.classList.add("text-red-500");
    61	      }
    62	    })
    63	    .catch((error) => {
    64	      console.log(error);
    65	      result.innerHTML = "Something went wrong!";
    66	    })
    67	    .then(function () {
    68	      form.reset();
    69	      setTimeout(() => {
    70	        result.style.display = "none";
    71	      }, 5000);
    72	    });
    73	});
    74	
    75	// end of contact form
    76

FILE: other/shop.js

     1	if (document.readyState == 'loading') {
     2	    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ready)
     3	} else {
     4	    ready()
     5	}
     6	
     7	function ready() {
     8	    var removeCartItemButtons = document.getElementsByClassName('btn-danger')
     9	    for (var i = 0; i < removeCartItemButtons.length; i++) {
    10	        var button = removeCartItemButtons[i]
    11	        button.addEventListener('click', removeCartItem)
    12	    }
    13	
    14	    var quantityInputs = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')
    15	    for (var i = 0; i < quantityInputs.length; i++) {
    16	        var input = quantityInputs[i]
    17	        input.addEventListener('change', quantityChanged)
    18	    }
    19	
    20	    var addToCartButtons = document.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-button')
    21	    for (var i = 0; i < addToCartButtons.length; i++) {
    22	        var button = addToCartButtons[i]
    23	        button.addEventListener('click', addToCartClicked)
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    document.getElementsByClassName('btn-purchase')[0].addEventListener('click', purchaseClicked)
    27	}
    28	
    29	function purchaseClicked() {
    30	    alert('Thank you for your purchase')
    31	    var cartItems = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    32	    while (cartItems.hasChildNodes()) {
    33	        cartItems.removeChild(cartItems.firstChild)
    34	    }
    35	    updateCartTotal()
    36	}
    37	
    38	function removeCartItem(event) {
    39	    var buttonClicked = event.target
    40	    buttonClicked.parentElement.parentElement.remove()
    41	    updateCartTotal()
    42	}
    43	
    44	function quantityChanged(event) {
    45	    var input = event.target
    46	    if (isNaN(input.value) || input.value <= 0) {
    47	        input.value = 1
    48	    }
    49	    updateCartTotal()
    50	}
    51	
    52	function addToCartClicked(event) {
    53	    var button = event.target
    54	    var shopItem = button.parentElement.parentElement
    55	    var title = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-title')[0].innerText
    56	    var price = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-price')[0].innerText
    57	    var imageSrc = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-image')[0].src
    58	    addItemToCart(title, price, imageSrc)
    59	    updateCartTotal()
    60	}
    61	
    62	function addItemToCart(title, price, imageSrc) {
    63	    var cartRow = document.createElement('div')
    64	    cartRow.classList.add('cart-row')
    65	    var cartItems = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    66	    var cartItemNames = cartItems.getElementsByClassName('cart-item-title')
    67	    for (var i = 0; i < cartItemNames.length; i++) {
    68	        if (cartItemNames[i].innerText == title) {
    69	            alert('This item is already added to the cart')
    70	            return
    71	        }
    72	    }
    73	    var cartRowContents = `
    74	        <div class="cart-item cart-column">
    75	            <img class="cart-item-image" src="${imageSrc}" width="100" height="100">
    76	            <span class="cart-item-title">${title}</span>
    77	        </div>
    78	        <span class="cart-price cart-column">${price}</span>
    79	        <div class="cart-quantity cart-column">
    80	            <input class="cart-quantity-input" type="number" value="1">
    81	            <button class="btn btn-danger" type="button">REMOVE</button>
    82	        </div>`
    83	    cartRow.innerHTML = cartRowContents
    84	    cartItems.append(cartRow)
    85	    cartRow.getElementsByClassName('btn-danger')[0].addEventListener('click', removeCartItem)
    86	    cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')[0].addEventListener('change', quantityChanged)
    87	}
    88	
    89	function updateCartTotal() {
    90	    var cartItemContainer = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    91	    var cartRows = cartItemContainer.getElementsByClassName('cart-row')
    92	    var total = 0
    93	    for (var i = 0; i < cartRows.length; i++) {
    94	        var cartRow = cartRows[i]
    95	        var priceElement = cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-price')[0]
    96	        var quantityElement = cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')[0]
    97	        var price = parseFloat(priceElement.innerText.replace('$', ''))
    98	        var quantity = quantityElement.value
    99	        total = total + (price * quantity)
   100	    }
   101	    total = Math.round(total * 100) / 100
   102	    document.getElementsByClassName('cart-total-price')[0].innerText = '$' + total
   103	}
